player.js
```
class Player {
  constructor(name, id) {
    this.id = id;
    this.name = name;
    this.health = 100;
    this.maxHealth = 100;
    this.level = 1;
    this.experience = 0;
    this.inventory = [];
    this.position = { x: 0, y: 0 };
    this.isAlive = true;
  }

  takeDamage(amount) {
    if (!this.isAlive) return;
    
    this.health = Math.max(0, this.health - amount);
    console.log(`${this.name} took ${amount} damage. Health: ${this.health}/${this.maxHealth}`);
    
    if (this.health <= 0) {
      this.die();
    }
  }

  heal(amount) {
    if (!this.isAlive) return;
    
    this.health = Math.min(this.maxHealth, this.health + amount);
    console.log(`${this.name} healed ${amount} HP. Health: ${this.health}/${this.maxHealth}`);
  }

  gainExperience(amount) {
    this.experience += amount;
    console.log(`${this.name} gained ${amount} XP. Total: ${this.experience}`);
    
    // Simple level up logic
    const xpForNextLevel = this.level * 100;
    if (this.experience >= xpForNextLevel) {
      this.levelUp();
    }
  }

  levelUp() {
    this.level++;
    this.experience = 0;
    this.maxHealth += 20;
    this.health = this.maxHealth;
    console.log(`🎉 ${this.name} leveled up to level ${this.level}! Max health increased to ${this.maxHealth}.`);
  }

  die() {
    this.isAlive = false;
    this.health = 0;
    console.log(`💀 ${this.name} has died!`);
    // Trigger death event, respawn logic, etc.
  }

  move(x, y) {
    this.position.x = x;
    this.position.y = y;
    console.log(`${this.name} moved to (${x}, ${y})`);
  }

  addItem(item) {
    this.inventory.push(item);
    console.log(`${this.name} picked up: ${item.name}`);
  }

  removeItem(itemName) {
    const index = this.inventory.findIndex(item => item.name === itemName);
    if (index !== -1) {
      const removed = this.inventory.splice(index, 1)[0];
      console.log(`${this.name} dropped: ${removed.name}`);
      return removed;
    }
    return null;
  }

  getStatus() {
    return {
      id: this.id,
      name: this.name,
      health: this.health,
      maxHealth: this.maxHealth,
      level: this.level,
      experience: this.experience,
      position: this.position,
      isAlive: this.isAlive,
      inventory: this.inventory.map(item => item.name)
    };
  }
}

// Export for Node.js / CommonJS
if (typeof module !== 'undefined' && module.exports) {
  module.exports = Player;
}

// Export for ES Modules
export default Player;
```

src/main.js
```
import * as THREE from 'three';
import { OrbitControls } from 'three/addons/controls/OrbitControls.js';

// Scene setup
const scene = new THREE.Scene();
scene.background = new THREE.Color(0x0a0a0f);

const camera = new THREE.PerspectiveCamera(60, window.innerWidth / window.innerHeight, 0.1, 100);
camera.position.set(0, 0, 5);

const renderer = new THREE.WebGLRenderer({ antialias: true, alpha: true });
renderer.setSize(window.innerWidth, window.innerHeight);
renderer.setPixelRatio(Math.min(window.devicePixelRatio, 2));
document.body.appendChild(renderer.domElement);

// Controls
const controls = new OrbitControls(camera, renderer.domElement);
controls.enableDamping = true;
controls.dampingFactor = 0.05;
controls.enablePan = false;
controls.minDistance = 3;
controls.maxDistance = 10;
controls.autoRotate = true;
controls.autoRotateSpeed = 0.3;

// Cube
const geometry = new THREE.BoxGeometry(2, 2, 2);
const material = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({
    color: 0x1a1a2e,
    roughness: 0.3,
    metalness: 0.2,
});
const cube = new THREE.Mesh(geometry, material);
scene.add(cube);

// Wireframe
const edges = new THREE.EdgesGeometry(geometry);
const lineMaterial = new THREE.LineBasicMaterial({ color: 0x00ffff, transparent: true, opacity: 0.3 });
const wireframe = new THREE.LineSegments(edges, lineMaterial);
cube.add(wireframe);

// Lighting
const ambientLight = new THREE.AmbientLight(0xffffff, 0.6);
scene.add(ambientLight);

const directionalLight = new THREE.DirectionalLight(0x00ffff, 0.8);
directionalLight.position.set(5, 10, 7);
scene.add(directionalLight);

const pointLight = new THREE.PointLight(0xff00ff, 0.5, 20);
pointLight.position.set(-5, 3, -5);
scene.add(pointLight);

// Minimal Audio Engine
class AudioEngine {
    constructor() {
        this.ctx = new (window.AudioContext || window.webkitAudioContext)();
        this.masterGain = this.ctx.createGain();
        this.masterGain.connect(this.ctx.destination);
        this.masterGain.gain.value = 0.15;
        this.isPlaying = false;
    }

    resume() {
        if (this.ctx.state === 'suspended') this.ctx.resume();
    }

    start() {
        if (this.isPlaying) return;
        this.isPlaying = true;
        this.scheduleLoop();
    }

    stop() {
        this.isPlaying = false;
    }

    scheduleLoop() {
        if (!this.isPlaying) return;
        
        const now = this.ctx.currentTime;
        const bpm = 70;
        const beatInterval = 60 / bpm;
        
        // Schedule 4 beats ahead
        for (let i = 0; i < 4; i++) {
            const time = now + i * beatInterval;
            this.scheduleBeat(time, i % 4);
        }
        
        // Schedule next loop
        setTimeout(() => this.scheduleLoop(), beatInterval * 4 * 1000 * 0.95);
    }

    scheduleBeat(time, beatIndex) {
        // Kick - deep and minimal
        if (beatIndex % 2 === 0) {
            const kick = this.ctx.createOscillator();
            const kickGain = this.ctx.createGain();
            kick.connect(kickGain);
            kickGain.connect(this.masterGain);
            kick.frequency.setValueAtTime(60, time);
            kick.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(30, time + 0.8);
            kickGain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.8, time);
            kickGain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, time + 0.8);
            kick.start(time);
            kick.stop(time + 1);
        }

        // Atmospheric pad - every 4 beats
        if (beatIndex === 0) {
            this.playPad(time);
        }

        // Subtle hi-hat on off-beats
        if (beatIndex % 2 === 1) {
            this.playHihat(time);
        }
    }

    playPad(time) {
        const frequencies = [65.41, 82.41, 98.00, 130.81]; // C2, E2, G2, C3
        frequencies.forEach((freq, i) => {
            const osc = this.ctx.createOscillator();
            const gain = this.ctx.createGain();
            const filter = this.ctx.createBiquadFilter();
            osc.connect(filter);
            filter.connect(gain);
            gain.connect(this.masterGain);
            
            osc.type = 'sine';
            osc.frequency.value = freq;
            filter.type = 'lowpass';
            filter.frequency.value = 400;
            filter.Q.value = 2;
            
            gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0, time);
            gain.gain.linearRampToValueAtTime(0.05, time + 0.5);
            gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, time + 6);
            
            osc.start(time);
            osc.stop(time + 7);
        });
    }

    playHihat(time) {
        const noise = this.ctx.createBufferSource();
        const bufferSize = this.ctx.sampleRate * 0.03;
        const buffer = this.ctx.createBuffer(1, bufferSize, this.ctx.sampleRate);
        const data = buffer.getChannelData(0);
        for (let i = 0; i < bufferSize; i++) data[i] = (Math.random() * 2 - 1) * 0.1;
        noise.buffer = buffer;
        
        const gain = this.ctx.createGain();
        const filter = this.ctx.createBiquadFilter();
        noise.connect(filter);
        filter.connect(gain);
        gain.connect(this.masterGain);
        
        filter.type = 'highpass';
        filter.frequency.value = 8000;
        gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.05, time);
        gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, time + 0.1);
        
        noise.start(time);
    }
}

const audioEngine = new AudioEngine();

// Start audio on first interaction
function initAudio() {
    audioEngine.resume();
    if (!audioEngine.isPlaying) audioEngine.start();
    window.removeEventListener('click', initAudio);
    window.removeEventListener('keydown', initAudio);
}
window.addEventListener('click', initAudio);
window.addEventListener('keydown', initAudio);

// Resize
window.addEventListener('resize', () => {
    camera.aspect = window.innerWidth / window.innerHeight;
    camera.updateProjectionMatrix();
    renderer.setSize(window.innerWidth, window.innerHeight);
});

// Animation loop
function animate() {
    requestAnimationFrame(animate);
    
    controls.update();
    
    // Subtle cube rotation (in addition to orbit controls auto-rotate)
    cube.rotation.x += 0.0005;
    cube.rotation.y += 0.0008;
    
    // Pulse wireframe opacity slightly
    const time = performance.now() * 0.001;
    wireframe.material.opacity = 0.2 + Math.sin(time * 0.5) * 0.1;
    
    renderer.render(scene, camera);
}

animate();
```

    renderer.setSize(window.innerWidth, window.innerHeight);

}

import * as THREE from 'three';
import { OrbitControls } from 'three/addons/controls/OrbitControls.js';

// Scene setup
const scene = new THREE.Scene();
scene.background = new THREE.Color(0xffffff); // White background

const camera = new THREE.PerspectiveCamera(60, window.innerWidth / window.innerHeight, 0.1, 100);
camera.position.set(0, 0, 5);

const renderer = new THREE.WebGLRenderer({ antialias: true, alpha: true });
renderer.setSize(window.innerWidth, window.innerHeight);
renderer.setPixelRatio(Math.min(window.devicePixelRatio, 2));
document.body.appendChild(renderer.domElement);

// Controls
const controls = new OrbitControls(camera, renderer.domElement);
controls.enableDamping = true;
controls.dampingFactor = 0.05;
controls.enablePan = false;
controls.minDistance = 3;
controls.maxDistance = 10;
controls.autoRotate = true;
controls.autoRotateSpeed = 0.3;

// Cube - black material with white specular highlights
const geometry = new THREE.BoxGeometry(2, 2, 2);
const material = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({
    color: 0x000000,        // Black base color
    roughness: 0.2,         // Low roughness for sharp highlights
    metalness: 0.1,         // Slight metalness for specular reflection
    envMapIntensity: 1.0,   // Environment map intensity
});
const cube = new THREE.Mesh(geometry, material);
scene.add(cube);

// Wireframe - subtle dark gray on white background
const edges = new THREE.EdgesGeometry(geometry);
const lineMaterial = new THREE.LineBasicMaterial({ 
    color: 0x333333, 
    transparent: true, 
    opacity: 0.4 
});
const wireframe = new THREE.LineSegments(edges, lineMaterial);
cube.add(wireframe);

// Lighting - white lights for white highlights on black cube
const ambientLight = new THREE.AmbientLight(0xffffff, 0.4); // Soft white ambient
scene.add(ambientLight);

// Main directional light - white, positioned for good highlights
const directionalLight = new THREE.DirectionalLight(0xffffff, 1.2);
directionalLight.position.set(5, 10, 7);
scene.add(directionalLight);

// Fill light - softer white from opposite side
const fillLight = new THREE.DirectionalLight(0xffffff, 0.5);
fillLight.position.set(-5, 5, -7);
scene.add(fillLight);

// Rim light - white from behind for edge highlights
const rimLight = new THREE.DirectionalLight(0xffffff, 0.8);
rimLight.position.set(0, -5, -10);
scene.add(rimLight);

// Minimal Audio Engine
class AudioEngine {
    constructor() {
        this.ctx = new (window.AudioContext || window.webkitAudioContext)();
        this.masterGain = this.ctx.createGain();
        this.masterGain.connect(this.ctx.destination);
        this.masterGain.gain.value = 0.15;
        this.isPlaying = false;
    }

    resume() {
        if (this.ctx.state === 'suspended') this.ctx.resume();
    }

    start() {
        if (this.isPlaying) return;
        this.isPlaying = true;
        this.scheduleLoop();
    }

    stop() {
        this.isPlaying = false;
    }

    scheduleLoop() {
        if (!this.isPlaying) return;
        
        const now = this.ctx.currentTime;
        const bpm = 70;
        const beatInterval = 60 / bpm;
        
        // Schedule 4 beats ahead
        for (let i = 0; i < 4; i++) {
            const time = now + i * beatInterval;
            this.scheduleBeat(time, i % 4);
        }
        
        // Schedule next loop
        setTimeout(() => this.scheduleLoop(), beatInterval * 4 * 1000 * 0.95);
    }

    scheduleBeat(time, beatIndex) {
        // Kick - deep and minimal
        if (beatIndex % 2 === 0) {
            const kick = this.ctx.createOscillator();
            const kickGain = this.ctx.createGain();
            kick.connect(kickGain);
            kickGain.connect(this.masterGain);
            kick.frequency.setValueAtTime(60, time);
            kick.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(30, time + 0.8);
            kickGain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.8, time);
            kickGain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, time + 0.8);
            kick.start(time);
            kick.stop(time + 1);
        }

        // Atmospheric pad - every 4 beats
        if (beatIndex === 0) {
            this.playPad(time);
        }

        // Subtle hi-hat on off-beats
        if (beatIndex % 2 === 1) {
            this.playHihat(time);
        }
    }

    playPad(time) {
        const frequencies = [65.41, 82.41, 98.00, 130.81]; // C2, E2, G2, C3
        frequencies.forEach((freq, i) => {
            const osc = this.ctx.createOscillator();
            const gain = this.ctx.createGain();
            const filter = this.ctx.createBiquadFilter();
            osc.connect(filter);
            filter.connect(gain);
            gain.connect(this.masterGain);
            
            osc.type = 'sine';
            osc.frequency.value = freq;
            filter.type = 'lowpass';
            filter.frequency.value = 400;
            filter.Q.value = 2;
            
            gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0, time);
            gain.gain.linearRampToValueAtTime(0.05, time + 0.5);
            gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, time + 6);
            
            osc.start(time);
            osc.stop(time + 7);
        });
    }

    playHihat(time) {
        const noise = this.ctx.createBufferSource();
        const bufferSize = this.ctx.sampleRate * 0.03;
        const buffer = this.ctx.createBuffer(1, bufferSize, this.ctx.sampleRate);
        const data = buffer.getChannelData(0);
        for (let i = 0; i < bufferSize; i++) data[i] = (Math.random() * 2 - 1) * 0.1;
        noise.buffer = buffer;
        
        const gain = this.ctx.createGain();
        const filter = this.ctx.createBiquadFilter();
        noise.connect(filter);
        filter.connect(gain);
        gain.connect(this.masterGain);
        
        filter.type = 'highpass';
        filter.frequency.value = 8000;
        gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.05, time);
        gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, time + 0.1);
        
        noise.start(time);
    }
}

const audioEngine = new AudioEngine();

// Start audio on first interaction
function initAudio() {
    audioEngine.resume();
    if (!audioEngine.isPlaying) audioEngine.start();
    window.removeEventListener('click', initAudio);
    window.removeEventListener('keydown', initAudio);
}
window.addEventListener('click', initAudio);
window.addEventListener('keydown', initAudio);

// Resize
window.addEventListener('resize', () => {
    camera.aspect = window.innerWidth / window.innerHeight;
    camera.updateProjectionMatrix();
    renderer.setSize(window.innerWidth, window.innerHeight);
});

// Animation loop
function animate() {
    requestAnimationFrame(animate);
    
    controls.update();
    
    // Subtle cube rotation (in addition to orbit controls auto-rotate)
    cube.rotation.x += 0.0005;
    cube.rotation.y += 0.0008;
    
    // Pulse wireframe opacity slightly
    const time = performance.now() * 0.001;
    wireframe.material.opacity = 0.3 + Math.sin(time * 0.5) * 0.1;
    
    renderer.render(scene, camera);
}

animate();
```

<thinking-content-7bbeb8e1441453ad999a0bbba8a46d4b>

The user wants to remove the visible reflection of the light sources (the "sun" or lamps) from the cube's faces, while keeping the specular highlights. In Three.js with MeshStandardMaterial, the specular highlights come from the lights, but if the material has metalness > 0, it will reflect the environment (which includes the lights if they're in the scene). However, the lights themselves are not visible as objects - they're just light sources. The reflection the user sees is likely the specular highlight from the directional lights.

To have highlights without the "reflection of the light source" looking like a distinct bright spot (like a sun reflection), we can:
1. Reduce metalness to 0 (non-metallic) so it doesn't mirror-reflect the environment
2. Use roughness to control highlight size
3. Use multiple softer lights instead of few bright ones
4. Possibly use a HemisphereLight for more natural ambient lighting

But the user specifically says "блики света должны быть, но само солнце или лампы его не видно вообще. То есть даже отражение его не видно." - they want highlights but no visible reflection of the light source.

With MeshStandardMaterial, the specular highlight IS the reflection of the light source. To make it less "sun-like" (a sharp distinct reflection), we need:
- Higher roughness to blur the highlight
- More lights from different directions to create a more uniform highlight
- Lower metalness (0) so it's dielectric specular, not metallic mirror reflection

- Set metalness: 0 (non-metal, dielectric)
- Increase roughness slightly to 0.3-0.4 for softer highlights
- Use more fill lights, less intense main light
- Remove the rim light that might create a distinct edge reflection

src/main.js
```
import * as THREE from 'three';
import { OrbitControls } from 'three/addons/controls/OrbitControls.js';

// Scene setup
const scene = new THREE.Scene();
scene.background = new THREE.Color(0xffffff); // White background

const camera = new THREE.PerspectiveCamera(60, window.innerWidth / window.innerHeight, 0.1, 100);
camera.position.set(0, 0, 5);

const renderer = new THREE.WebGLRenderer({ antialias: true, alpha: true });
renderer.setSize(window.innerWidth, window.innerHeight);
renderer.setPixelRatio(Math.min(window.devicePixelRatio, 2));
document.body.appendChild(renderer.domElement);

// Controls
const controls = new OrbitControls(camera, renderer.domElement);
controls.enableDamping = true;
controls.dampingFactor = 0.05;
controls.enablePan = false;
controls.minDistance = 3;
controls.maxDistance = 10;
controls.autoRotate = true;
controls.autoRotateSpeed = 0.3;

// Create cube as 6 separate face meshes
const faceSize = 2;
const halfSize = faceSize / 2;
const faces = [];

// Face data: [position, rotation, normal]
const faceData = [
    // Front (+Z)
    { pos: [0, 0, halfSize], rot: [0, 0, 0], normal: [0, 0, 1] },
    // Back (-Z)
    { pos: [0, 0, -halfSize], rot: [0, Math.PI, 0], normal: [0, 0, -1] },
    // Right (+X)
    { pos: [halfSize, 0, 0], rot: [0, -Math.PI / 2, 0], normal: [1, 0, 0] },
    // Left (-X)
    { pos: [-halfSize, 0, 0], rot: [0, Math.PI / 2, 0], normal: [-1, 0, 0] },
    // Top (+Y)
    { pos: [0, halfSize, 0], rot: [-Math.PI / 2, 0, 0], normal: [0, 1, 0] },
    // Bottom (-Y)
    { pos: [0, -halfSize, 0], rot: [Math.PI / 2, 0, 0], normal: [0, -1, 0] },
];

const faceGeometry = new THREE.PlaneGeometry(faceSize, faceSize);
const faceMaterial = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({
    color: 0x000000,
    roughness: 0.35,
    metalness: 0.0,
    side: THREE.DoubleSide, // Visible from both sides
});

faceData.forEach((data, index) => {
    const face = new THREE.Mesh(faceGeometry, faceMaterial);
    face.position.set(...data.pos);
    face.rotation.set(...data.rot);
    face.userData = { faceIndex: index, originalPosition: data.pos, originalRotation: data.rot };
    scene.add(face);
    faces.push(face);
});

// Wireframe - create edges for the whole cube
const cubeGeometry = new THREE.BoxGeometry(faceSize, faceSize, faceSize);
const edges = new THREE.EdgesGeometry(cubeGeometry);
const lineMaterial = new THREE.LineBasicMaterial({ 
    color: 0x333333, 
    transparent: true, 
    opacity: 0.4 
});
const wireframe = new THREE.LineSegments(edges, lineMaterial);
scene.add(wireframe);

// Group for rotation (contains faces + wireframe)
const cubeGroup = new THREE.Group();
faces.forEach(face => cubeGroup.add(face));
cubeGroup.add(wireframe);
scene.add(cubeGroup);

// Lighting - multiple soft white lights
const ambientLight = new THREE.AmbientLight(0xffffff, 0.5);
scene.add(ambientLight);

const directionalLight = new THREE.DirectionalLight(0xffffff, 0.7);
directionalLight.position.set(5, 10, 7);
scene.add(directionalLight);

const fillLight = new THREE.DirectionalLight(0xffffff, 0.5);
fillLight.position.set(-5, 5, -7);
scene.add(fillLight);

const topLight = new THREE.DirectionalLight(0xffffff, 0.4);
topLight.position.set(0, 10, 0);
scene.add(topLight);

const sideLight1 = new THREE.DirectionalLight(0xffffff, 0.3);
sideLight1.position.set(10, 0, 5);
scene.add(sideLight1);

const sideLight2 = new THREE.DirectionalLight(0xffffff, 0.3);
sideLight2.position.set(-10, 0, -5);
scene.add(sideLight2);

// Raycaster for click detection
const raycaster = new THREE.Raycaster();
const mouse = new THREE.Vector2();

// Click handler
function onClick(event) {
    // Calculate mouse position in normalized device coordinates
    mouse.x = (event.clientX / window.innerWidth) * 2 - 1;
    mouse.y = -(event.clientY / window.innerHeight) * 2 + 1;

    // Raycast against face meshes only
    raycaster.setFromCamera(mouse, camera);
    const intersects = raycaster.intersectObjects(faces.filter(f => f.visible), true);

    if (intersects.length > 0) {
        const hitFace = intersects[0].object;
        // Animate face disappearance
        animateFaceOut(hitFace);
    }
}

function animateFaceOut(face) {
    const startTime = performance.now();
    const duration = 300; // ms
    const startScale = 1;
    const targetScale = 0.01;
    const startOpacity = face.material.opacity !== undefined ? face.material.opacity : 1;

    function animate() {
        const elapsed = performance.now() - startTime;
        const progress = Math.min(elapsed / duration, 1);
        const eased = 1 - Math.pow(1 - progress, 3); // easeOutCubic

        face.scale.setScalar(startScale + (targetScale - startScale) * eased);
        face.material.opacity = startOpacity * (1 - eased);
        face.material.transparent = true;
        face.material.needsUpdate = true;

        if (progress < 1) {
            requestAnimationFrame(animate);
        } else {
            face.visible = false;
            face.scale.setScalar(1);
            face.material.opacity = startOpacity;
            face.material.transparent = false;
        }
    }
    animate();
}

window.addEventListener('click', onClick);

// Minimal Audio Engine
class AudioEngine {
    constructor() {
        this.ctx = new (window.AudioContext || window.webkitAudioContext)();
        this.masterGain = this.ctx.createGain();
        this.masterGain.connect(this.ctx.destination);
        this.masterGain.gain.value = 0.15;
        this.isPlaying = false;
    }

    resume() {
        if (this.ctx.state === 'suspended') this.ctx.resume();
    }

    start() {
        if (this.isPlaying) return;
        this.isPlaying = true;
        this.scheduleLoop();
    }

    stop() {
        this.isPlaying = false;
    }

    scheduleLoop() {
        if (!this.isPlaying) return;
        
        const now = this.ctx.currentTime;
        const bpm = 70;
        const beatInterval = 60 / bpm;
        
        for (let i = 0; i < 4; i++) {
            const time = now + i * beatInterval;
            this.scheduleBeat(time, i % 4);
        }
        
        setTimeout(() => this.scheduleLoop(), beatInterval * 4 * 1000 * 0.95);
    }

    scheduleBeat(time, beatIndex) {
        if (beatIndex % 2 === 0) {
            const kick = this.ctx.createOscillator();
            const kickGain = this.ctx.createGain();
            kick.connect(kickGain);
            kickGain.connect(this.masterGain);
            kick.frequency.setValueAtTime(60, time);
            kick.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(30, time + 0.8);
            kickGain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.8, time);
            kickGain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, time + 0.8);
            kick.start(time);
            kick.stop(time + 1);
        }

        if (beatIndex === 0) {
            this.playPad(time);
        }

        if (beatIndex % 2 === 1) {
            this.playHihat(time);
        }
    }

    playPad(time) {
        const frequencies = [65.41, 82.41, 98.00, 130.81];
        frequencies.forEach((freq) => {
            const osc = this.ctx.createOscillator();
            const gain = this.ctx.createGain();
            const filter = this.ctx.createBiquadFilter();
            osc.connect(filter);
            filter.connect(gain);
            gain.connect(this.masterGain);
            
            osc.type = 'sine';
            osc.frequency.value = freq;
            filter.type = 'lowpass';
            filter.frequency.value = 400;
            filter.Q.value = 2;
            
            gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0, time);
            gain.gain.linearRampToValueAtTime(0.05, time + 0.5);
            gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, time + 6);
            
            osc.start(time);
            osc.stop(time + 7);
        });
    }

    playHihat(time) {
        const noise = this.ctx.createBufferSource();
        const bufferSize = this.ctx.sampleRate * 0.03;
        const buffer = this.ctx.createBuffer(1, bufferSize, this.ctx.sampleRate);
        const data = buffer.getChannelData(0);
        for (let i = 0; i < bufferSize; i++) data[i] = (Math.random() * 2 - 1) * 0.1;
        noise.buffer = buffer;
        
        const gain = this.ctx.createGain();
        const filter = this.ctx.createBiquadFilter();
        noise.connect(filter);
        filter.connect(gain);
        gain.connect(this.masterGain);
        
        filter.type = 'highpass';
        filter.frequency.value = 8000;
        gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.05, time);
        gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, time + 0.1);
        
        noise.start(time);
    }
}

const audioEngine = new AudioEngine();

function initAudio() {
    audioEngine.resume();
    if (!audioEngine.isPlaying) audioEngine.start();
    window.removeEventListener('click', initAudio);
    window.removeEventListener('keydown', initAudio);
}
window.addEventListener('click', initAudio);
window.addEventListener('keydown', initAudio);

// Resize
window.addEventListener('resize', () => {
    camera.aspect = window.innerWidth / window.innerHeight;
    camera.updateProjectionMatrix();
    renderer.setSize(window.innerWidth, window.innerHeight);
});

// Animation loop
function animate() {
    requestAnimationFrame(animate);
    
    controls.update();
    
    // Subtle cube rotation
    cubeGroup.rotation.x += 0.0005;
    cubeGroup.rotation.y += 0.0008;
    
    // Pulse wireframe opacity slightly
    const time = performance.now() * 0.001;
    wireframe.material.opacity = 0.3 + Math.sin(time * 0.5) * 0.1;
    
    renderer.render(scene, camera);
}

animate();

<thinking-content-7bbeb8e1441453ad999a0bbba8a46d4b>

The issue is that when animating the face out, the material is shared across all faces. When we modify `face.material.opacity` and `face.material.transparent`, it affects all faces because they all use the same material instance.

I need to give each face its own material instance, or clone the material when animating. The simplest fix is to create a unique material for each face when creating them.

    face.material.opacity = newOpacity;
    face.material.transparent = newOpacity < 1;
    face.material.needsUpdate = true;

    if (userData.clickCount >= userData.maxClicks) {

        face.material.opacity = startOpacity * (1 - eased);
        face.material.transparent = true;
        face.material.needsUpdate = true;

        if (progress < 1) {
            requestAnimationFrame(animate);

            face.scale.setScalar(1);
            face.material.opacity = 1;
            face.material.transparent = false;
            face.userData.clickCount = 0; // Reset for potential future use
        }

}

        if (!this.isPlaying) return;
        
        const now = this.ctx.currentTime;
        const bpm = 70;
        const beatInterval = 60 / bpm;
        
        for (let i = 0; i < 4; i++) {
            const time = now + i * beatInterval;
            this.scheduleBeat(time, i % 4);
        }
        
        setTimeout(() => this.scheduleLoop(), beatInterval * 4 * 1000 * 0.95);
    }

    scheduleBeat(time, beatIndex) {
        if (beatIndex % 2 === 0) {
            const kick = this.ctx.createOscillator();
            const kickGain = this.ctx.createGain();
            kick.connect(kickGain);
            kickGain.connect(this.masterGain);
            kick.frequency.setValueAtTime(60, time);
            kick.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(30, time + 0.8);
            kickGain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.8, time);
            kickGain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, time + 0.8);
            kick.start(time);
            kick.stop(time + 1);
        }

        if (beatIndex === 0) {
            this.playPad(time);
        }

        if (beatIndex % 2 === 1) {
            this.playHihat(time);
        }
    }

    playPad(time) {
        const frequencies = [65.41, 82.41, 98.00, 130.81];
        frequencies.forEach((freq) => {
            const osc = this.ctx.createOscillator();
            const gain = this.ctx.createGain();
            const filter = this.ctx.createBiquadFilter();
            osc.connect(filter);
            filter.connect(gain);
            gain.connect(this.masterGain);
            
            osc.type = 'sine';
            osc.frequency.value = freq;
            filter.type = 'lowpass';
            filter.frequency.value = 400;
            filter.Q.value = 2;
            
            gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0, time);
            gain.gain.linearRampToValueAtTime(0.05, time + 0.5);
            gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, time + 6);
            
            osc.start(time);
            osc.stop(time + 7);
        });
    }

    playHihat(time) {
        const noise = this.ctx.createBufferSource();
        const bufferSize = this.ctx.sampleRate * 0.03;
        const buffer = this.ctx.createBuffer(1, bufferSize, this.ctx.sampleRate);
        const data = buffer.getChannelData(0);
        for (let i = 0; i < bufferSize; i++) data[i] = (Math.random() * 2 - 1) * 0.1;
        noise.buffer = buffer;
        
        const gain = this.ctx.createGain();
        const filter = this.ctx.createBiquadFilter();
        noise.connect(filter);
        filter.connect(gain);
        gain.connect(this.masterGain);
        
        filter.type = 'highpass';
        filter.frequency.value = 8000;
        gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.05, time);
        gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, time + 0.1);
        
        noise.start(time);
    }
}

const audioEngine = new AudioEngine();

function initAudio() {
    audioEngine.resume();
    if (!audioEngine.isPlaying) audioEngine.start();
    window.removeEventListener('click', initAudio);
    window.removeEventListener('keydown', initAudio);
}
window.addEventListener('click', initAudio);
window.addEventListener('keydown', initAudio);

}

2. On each click, add more cracks to the face

Since we're using Three.js, I can either:
- Use a canvas texture that I draw cracks on
- Create crack geometry (lines) and add them to the face
- Use a pre-made crack texture and blend it in progressively

The canvas texture approach seems most flexible - I can draw procedural cracks on a canvas and use it as an alpha map or opacity map for the face material.

    // Left (-X)
    { pos: [-halfSize, 0, 0], rot: [0, Math.PI / 2, 0], normal: [-1, 0, 0] },
    // Top (+Y)
    { pos: [0, halfSize, 0], rot: [-Math.PI / 2, 0, 0], normal: [0, 1, 0] },
    // Bottom (-Y)
    { pos: [0, -halfSize, 0], rot: [Math.PI / 2, 0, 0], normal: [0, -1, 0] },
];

const faceGeometry = new THREE.PlaneGeometry(faceSize, faceSize);

// Crack texture generator
class CrackTexture {
    constructor(size = 512) {
        this.size = size;
        this.canvas = document.createElement('canvas');
        this.canvas.width = size;
        this.canvas.height = size;
        this.ctx = this.canvas.getContext('2d');
        this.cracks = []; // Array of crack paths
        this.texture = new THREE.CanvasTexture(this.canvas);
        this.texture.wrapS = THREE.ClampToEdgeWrapping;
        this.texture.wrapT = THREE.ClampToEdgeWrapping;
        this.clear();
    }

    clear() {
        this.ctx.fillStyle = 'white'; // White = opaque (no cracks)
        this.ctx.fillRect(0, 0, this.size, this.size);
        this.cracks = [];
        this.texture.needsUpdate = true;
    }

    // Generate a lightning-like crack path
    generateCrack() {
        const path = [];
        const startX = Math.random() * this.size;
        const startY = Math.random() * this.size;
        path.push({ x: startX, y: startY });

        const segments = 8 + Math.floor(Math.random() * 8);
        let x = startX;
        let y = startY;
        let angle = Math.random() * Math.PI * 2;

        for (let i = 0; i < segments; i++) {
            const length = 20 + Math.random() * 40;
            angle += (Math.random() - 0.5) * 1.2; // Random walk
            x += Math.cos(angle) * length;
            y += Math.sin(angle) * length;

            // Bounce off edges
            if (x < 0) { x = -x; angle = Math.PI - angle; }
            if (x > this.size) { x = 2 * this.size - x; angle = Math.PI - angle; }
            if (y < 0) { y = -y; angle = -angle; }
            if (y > this.size) { y = 2 * this.size - y; angle = -angle; }

            path.push({ x, y });

            // Occasionally branch
            if (Math.random() < 0.15 && i > 2) {
                const branchAngle = angle + (Math.random() - 0.5) * Math.PI;
                const branchLength = 15 + Math.random() * 30;
                const bx = x + Math.cos(branchAngle) * branchLength;
                const by = y + Math.sin(branchAngle) * branchLength;
                this.cracks.push([{ x, y }, { x: bx, y: by }]);
            }
        }
        this.cracks.push(path);
    }

    // Add N new cracks and redraw
    addCracks(count) {
        for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
            this.generateCrack();
        }
        this.redraw();
    }

    redraw() {
        this.ctx.fillStyle = 'white';
        this.ctx.fillRect(0, 0, this.size, this.size);

        this.ctx.strokeStyle = 'black'; // Black = transparent (cracks)
        this.ctx.lineWidth = 1.5;
        this.ctx.lineCap = 'round';
        this.ctx.lineJoin = 'round';

        // Draw main cracks
        this.cracks.forEach(path => {
            if (path.length < 2) return;
            this.ctx.beginPath();
            this.ctx.moveTo(path[0].x, path[0].y);
            for (let i = 1; i < path.length; i++) {
                this.ctx.lineTo(path[i].x, path[i].y);
            }
            this.ctx.stroke();
        });

        // Add fine detail cracks
        this.ctx.lineWidth = 0.8;
        this.cracks.forEach(path => {
            if (path.length < 3) return;
            for (let i = 1; i < path.length - 1; i += 2) {
                if (Math.random() < 0.3) {
                    const px = path[i].x;
                    const py = path[i].y;
                    const angle = Math.random() * Math.PI * 2;
                    const len = 5 + Math.random() * 15;
                    this.ctx.beginPath();
                    this.ctx.moveTo(px, py);
                    this.ctx.lineTo(px + Math.cos(angle) * len, py + Math.sin(angle) * len);
                    this.ctx.stroke();
                }
            }
        });

        this.texture.needsUpdate = true;
    }

    getTexture() {
        return this.texture;
    }

    getCrackCount() {
        return this.cracks.length;
    }
}

faceData.forEach((data, index) => {
    // Each face gets its own material with unique crack texture
    const crackTexture = new CrackTexture(512);

    });

    face.position.set(...data.pos);
    face.rotation.set(...data.rot);
    face.userData = { 
        faceIndex: index, 
        originalPosition: data.pos, 
        originalRotation: data.rot,
        clickCount: 0,
        maxClicks: 4,
        crackTexture: crackTexture,
    };
    scene.add(face);
    faces.push(face);
});

// Wireframe - create edges for the whole cube

const edges = new THREE.EdgesGeometry(cubeGeometry);
const lineMaterial = new THREE.LineBasicMaterial({ 
    color: 0x333333, 
    transparent: true, 
    opacity: 0.4 
});
const wireframe = new THREE.LineSegments(edges, lineMaterial);
scene.add(wireframe);

// Group for rotation (contains faces + wireframe)

faces.forEach(face => cubeGroup.add(face));
cubeGroup.add(wireframe);
scene.add(cubeGroup);

// Lighting - multiple soft white lights

scene.add(ambientLight);

directionalLight.position.set(5, 10, 7);

sideLight2.position.set(-10, 0, -5);

// Raycaster for click detection
const raycaster = new THREE.Raycaster();
const mouse = new THREE.Vector2();

// Click handler
function onClick(event) {
    mouse.x = (event.clientX / window.innerWidth) * 2 - 1;
    mouse.y = -(event.clientY / window.innerHeight) * 2 + 1;

    raycaster.setFromCamera(mouse, camera);
    const intersects = raycaster.intersectObjects(faces.filter(f => f.visible), true);

    if (intersects.length > 0) {
        const hitFace = intersects[0].object;
        handleFaceClick(hitFace);
    }
}

function handleFaceClick(face) {
    const userData = face.userData;
    userData.clickCount++;
    
    // Add more cracks with each click
    // Click 1: 3 cracks, Click 2: 5 cracks, Click 3: 8 cracks, Click 4: shatter
    const cracksToAdd = [3, 5, 8, 0][userData.clickCount - 1] || 0;
    if (cracksToAdd > 0) {
        userData.crackTexture.addCracks(cracksToAdd);
    }
    
    // If 4th click, animate shatter disappearance
    if (userData.clickCount >= userData.maxClicks) {
        animateFaceShatter(face);
    }
}

function animateFaceShatter(face) {
    const userData = face.userData;
    const crackTexture = userData.crackTexture;
    
    // Create shatter pieces from the crack pattern
    const pieces = createShatterPieces(face, crackTexture);
    
    // Animate pieces flying off
    const startTime = performance.now();
    const duration = 600;
    
    function animatePieces() {
        const elapsed = performance.now() - startTime;
        const progress = Math.min(elapsed / duration, 1);
        const eased = Math.pow(progress, 2); // easeInQuad
        
        pieces.forEach((piece, i) => {
            const delay = i * 0.02;
            const pieceProgress = Math.max(0, (progress - delay) / (1 - delay));
            if (pieceProgress <= 0) return;
            
            const pieceEased = Math.pow(pieceProgress, 2);
            
            // Fly outward and rotate
            piece.position.x += piece.userData.velocity.x * pieceEased * 0.5;
            piece.position.y += piece.userData.velocity.y * pieceEased * 0.5;
            piece.position.z += piece.userData.velocity.z * pieceEased * 0.5;
            
            piece.rotation.x += piece.userData.angularVelocity.x * pieceEased;
            piece.rotation.y += piece.userData.angularVelocity.y * pieceEased;
            piece.rotation.z += piece.userData.angularVelocity.z * pieceEased;
            
            // Fade out
            piece.material.opacity = 1 - pieceEased;
            piece.material.transparent = true;
            piece.material.needsUpdate = true;
        });
        
        if (progress < 1) {
            requestAnimationFrame(animatePieces);
        } else {
            // Cleanup
            pieces.forEach(piece => {
                scene.remove(piece);
                piece.geometry.dispose();
                piece.material.dispose();
            });
            face.visible = false;
            face.material.alphaMap = crackTexture.getTexture(); // Restore original texture
            crackTexture.clear(); // Reset for potential reuse
            userData.clickCount = 0;
        }
    }
    
    // Hide original face immediately
    face.visible = false;
    animatePieces();
}

function createShatterPieces(face, crackTexture) {
    const pieces = [];
    const pieceCount = 12 + Math.floor(Math.random() * 8);
    
    // Get face world position and rotation
    const worldPos = new THREE.Vector3();
    const worldQuat = new THREE.Quaternion();
    face.getWorldPosition(worldPos);
    face.getWorldQuaternion(worldQuat);
    
    for (let i = 0; i < pieceCount; i++) {
        // Create irregular piece geometry (slightly randomized box)
        const size = 0.15 + Math.random() * 0.15;
        const geo = new THREE.BoxGeometry(size, size, 0.02);
        
        // Use face material but with crack texture
        const mat = face.material.clone();
        mat.alphaMap = crackTexture.getTexture();
        mat.transparent = true;
        mat.side = THREE.DoubleSide;
        mat.needsUpdate = true;
        
        const piece = new THREE.Mesh(geo, mat);
        
        // Position near face center with small offset
        const offset = new THREE.Vector3(
            (Math.random() - 0.5) * 0.8,
            (Math.random() - 0.5) * 0.8,
            0
        );
        offset.applyQuaternion(worldQuat);
        piece.position.copy(worldPos).add(offset);
        piece.quaternion.copy(worldQuat);
        
        // Random velocity outward
        const velocity = new THREE.Vector3(
            (Math.random() - 0.5) * 0.02,
            (Math.random() - 0.5) * 0.02,
            (Math.random() - 0.5) * 0.01 - 0.005 // Slight backward
        );
        velocity.applyQuaternion(worldQuat);
        
        // Random angular velocity
        const angularVelocity = new THREE.Vector3(
            (Math.random() - 0.5) * 0.05,
            (Math.random() - 0.5) * 0.05,
            (Math.random() - 0.5) * 0.05
        );
        
        piece.userData = { velocity, angularVelocity };
        scene.add(piece);
        pieces.push(piece);
    }
    
    return pieces;
}

#### Сейчас эти линии, которые появляются на грани в приклике, полностью прозрачны. Из-за этого их не видно, когда с противоположной стороны грань куба черная. Пусть эти линии будут хотя бы серого цвета или приближенного к такому, как трещина на стекле. Они должны быть видны на черном кубе всякий раз, когда я с какой бы стороны ни посмотрел.  

<thinking-content-7bbeb8e1441453ad999a0bbba8a46d4b>

The user wants the cracks to be visible on the black cube from all angles. Currently, the cracks are implemented using an alphaMap (transparency), which makes them fully transparent. On a black cube against a white background, this means you can see through the cracks to the white background, but when viewing from the back (where the cube face is black), the cracks are invisible because they're just transparent holes in a black surface.

The solution is to render the cracks as actual geometry or as a visible texture (color) rather than transparency. Since the cube faces are black, we need the cracks to be a lighter color (gray/white) to be visible.

Options:
1. Use a color map (map) instead of alphaMap to draw gray/white cracks on the black surface
2. Use both - keep alphaMap for the shatter effect but also add a visible crack texture
3. Create actual 3D geometry for cracks (too complex)

Best approach: Modify the CrackTexture to generate a visible texture (gray cracks on black) and use it as the `map` (color map) instead of/in addition to `alphaMap`. For the shatter effect, we still need transparency, so we can keep alphaMap but make the cracks visible in the color map.

Actually, the simplest fix: Change the crack texture to draw gray/white lines on a black background, and use it as the `map` (diffuse map) instead of `alphaMap`. This way the cracks are visible as lighter lines on the black face from all angles.

But wait - the shatter effect uses the alphaMap to create transparent pieces. We need both:
- Visible cracks on the intact face (color map)
- Transparency for shatter pieces (alpha map)

We can generate two textures: one for color (gray cracks on black) and one for alpha (white background with black cracks for transparency). Or we can use the same texture for both but interpret differently.

import * as THREE from 'three';
import { OrbitControls } from 'three/addons/controls/OrbitControls.js';

// Scene setup
const scene = new THREE.Scene();
scene.background = new THREE.Color(0xffffff); // White background

const camera = new THREE.PerspectiveCamera(60, window.innerWidth / window.innerHeight, 0.1, 100);
camera.position.set(0, 0, 5);

const renderer = new THREE.WebGLRenderer({ antialias: true, alpha: true });
renderer.setSize(window.innerWidth, window.innerHeight);
renderer.setPixelRatio(Math.min(window.devicePixelRatio, 2));
document.body.appendChild(renderer.domElement);

// Controls
const controls = new OrbitControls(camera, renderer.domElement);
controls.enableDamping = true;
controls.dampingFactor = 0.05;
controls.enablePan = false;
controls.minDistance = 3;
controls.maxDistance = 10;
controls.autoRotate = true;
controls.autoRotateSpeed = 0.3;

// Create cube as 6 separate face meshes
const faceSize = 2;
const halfSize = faceSize / 2;
const faces = [];

// Face data: [position, rotation, normal]
const faceData = [
    // Front (+Z)
    { pos: [0, 0, halfSize], rot: [0, 0, 0], normal: [0, 0, 1] },
    // Back (-Z)
    { pos: [0, 0, -halfSize], rot: [0, Math.PI, 0], normal: [0, 0, -1] },
    // Right (+X)
    { pos: [halfSize, 0, 0], rot: [0, -Math.PI / 2, 0], normal: [1, 0, 0] },
    // Left (-X)
    { pos: [-halfSize, 0, 0], rot: [0, Math.PI / 2, 0], normal: [-1, 0, 0] },
    // Top (+Y)
    { pos: [0, halfSize, 0], rot: [-Math.PI / 2, 0, 0], normal: [0, 1, 0] },
    // Bottom (-Y)
    { pos: [0, -halfSize, 0], rot: [Math.PI / 2, 0, 0], normal: [0, -1, 0] },
];

const faceGeometry = new THREE.PlaneGeometry(faceSize, faceSize);

// Crack texture generator - produces both visible (map) and alpha (alphaMap) textures
class CrackTexture {
    constructor(size = 512) {
        this.size = size;
        
        // Visible texture: black background with gray/white cracks (for map)
        this.visibleCanvas = document.createElement('canvas');
        this.visibleCanvas.width = size;
        this.visibleCanvas.height = size;
        this.visibleCtx = this.visibleCanvas.getContext('2d');
        this.visibleTexture = new THREE.CanvasTexture(this.visibleCanvas);
        this.visibleTexture.wrapS = THREE.ClampToEdgeWrapping;
        this.visibleTexture.wrapT = THREE.ClampToEdgeWrapping;
        
        // Alpha texture: white background with black cracks (for alphaMap on shatter pieces)
        this.alphaCanvas = document.createElement('canvas');
        this.alphaCanvas.width = size;
        this.alphaCanvas.height = size;
        this.alphaCtx = this.alphaCanvas.getContext('2d');
        this.alphaTexture = new THREE.CanvasTexture(this.alphaCanvas);
        this.alphaTexture.wrapS = THREE.ClampToEdgeWrapping;
        this.alphaTexture.wrapT = THREE.ClampToEdgeWrapping;
        
        this.cracks = []; // Array of crack paths
        this.clear();
    }

    clear() {
        // Visible: black background (matches cube face color)
        this.visibleCtx.fillStyle = '#000000';
        this.visibleCtx.fillRect(0, 0, this.size, this.size);
        
        // Alpha: white background (opaque)
        this.alphaCtx.fillStyle = 'white';
        this.alphaCtx.fillRect(0, 0, this.size, this.size);
        
        this.cracks = [];
        this.visibleTexture.needsUpdate = true;
        this.alphaTexture.needsUpdate = true;
    }

    // Generate a lightning-like crack path
    generateCrack() {
        const path = [];
        const startX = Math.random() * this.size;
        const startY = Math.random() * this.size;
        path.push({ x: startX, y: startY });

        const segments = 8 + Math.floor(Math.random() * 8);
        let x = startX;
        let y = startY;
        let angle = Math.random() * Math.PI * 2;

        for (let i = 0; i < segments; i++) {
            const length = 20 + Math.random() * 40;
            angle += (Math.random() - 0.5) * 1.2; // Random walk
            x += Math.cos(angle) * length;
            y += Math.sin(angle) * length;

            // Bounce off edges
            if (x < 0) { x = -x; angle = Math.PI - angle; }
            if (x > this.size) { x = 2 * this.size - x; angle = Math.PI - angle; }
            if (y < 0) { y = -y; angle = -angle; }
            if (y > this.size) { y = 2 * this.size - y; angle = -angle; }

            path.push({ x, y });

            // Occasionally branch
            if (Math.random() < 0.15 && i > 2) {
                const branchAngle = angle + (Math.random() - 0.5) * Math.PI;
                const branchLength = 15 + Math.random() * 30;
                const bx = x + Math.cos(branchAngle) * branchLength;
                const by = y + Math.sin(branchAngle) * branchLength;
                this.cracks.push([{ x, y }, { x: bx, y: by }]);
            }
        }
        this.cracks.push(path);
    }

    // Add N new cracks and redraw
    addCracks(count) {
        for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
            this.generateCrack();
        }
        this.redraw();
    }

    redraw() {
        // Visible texture: black bg, gray/white cracks
        this.visibleCtx.fillStyle = '#000000';
        this.visibleCtx.fillRect(0, 0, this.size, this.size);

        // Alpha texture: white bg, black cracks
        this.alphaCtx.fillStyle = 'white';
        this.alphaCtx.fillRect(0, 0, this.size, this.size);

        // Draw main cracks
        this.cracks.forEach(path => {
            if (path.length < 2) return;
            
            // Visible: draw gray/white crack lines
            this.visibleCtx.strokeStyle = '#666666'; // Medium gray - visible on black
            this.visibleCtx.lineWidth = 2.0;
            this.visibleCtx.lineCap = 'round';
            this.visibleCtx.lineJoin = 'round';
            this.visibleCtx.beginPath();
            this.visibleCtx.moveTo(path[0].x, path[0].y);
            for (let i = 1; i < path.length; i++) {
                this.visibleCtx.lineTo(path[i].x, path[i].y);
            }
            this.visibleCtx.stroke();
            
            // Add highlight for glass-like effect
            this.visibleCtx.strokeStyle = '#aaaaaa'; // Lighter highlight
            this.visibleCtx.lineWidth = 0.8;
            this.visibleCtx.beginPath();
            this.visibleCtx.moveTo(path[0].x, path[0].y);
            for (let i = 1; i < path.length; i++) {
                this.visibleCtx.lineTo(path[i].x, path[i].y);
            }
            this.visibleCtx.stroke();

            // Alpha: draw black crack lines (transparent)
            this.alphaCtx.strokeStyle = 'black';
            this.alphaCtx.lineWidth = 2.5; // Slightly wider for clean edges
            this.alphaCtx.lineCap = 'round';
            this.alphaCtx.lineJoin = 'round';
            this.alphaCtx.beginPath();
            this.alphaCtx.moveTo(path[0].x, path[0].y);
            for (let i = 1; i < path.length; i++) {
                this.alphaCtx.lineTo(path[i].x, path[i].y);
            }
            this.alphaCtx.stroke();
        });

        // Add fine detail cracks
        this.cracks.forEach(path => {
            if (path.length < 3) return;
            for (let i = 1; i < path.length - 1; i += 2) {
                if (Math.random() < 0.3) {
                    const px = path[i].x;
                    const py = path[i].y;
                    const angle = Math.random() * Math.PI * 2;
                    const len = 5 + Math.random() * 15;
                    
                    // Visible detail
                    this.visibleCtx.strokeStyle = '#555555';
                    this.visibleCtx.lineWidth = 1.0;
                    this.visibleCtx.beginPath();
                    this.visibleCtx.moveTo(px, py);
                    this.visibleCtx.lineTo(px + Math.cos(angle) * len, py + Math.sin(angle) * len);
                    this.visibleCtx.stroke();
                    
                    // Alpha detail
                    this.alphaCtx.strokeStyle = 'black';
                    this.alphaCtx.lineWidth = 1.5;
                    this.alphaCtx.beginPath();
                    this.alphaCtx.moveTo(px, py);
                    this.alphaCtx.lineTo(px + Math.cos(angle) * len, py + Math.sin(angle) * len);
                    this.alphaCtx.stroke();
                }
            }
        });

        this.visibleTexture.needsUpdate = true;
        this.alphaTexture.needsUpdate = true;
    }

    getVisibleTexture() {
        return this.visibleTexture;
    }

    getAlphaTexture() {
        return this.alphaTexture;
    }

    getCrackCount() {
        return this.cracks.length;
    }
}

faceData.forEach((data, index) => {
    // Each face gets its own crack texture generator
    const crackTexture = new CrackTexture(512);
    
    // Main face material: use visible texture as map (color), no transparency
    const faceMaterial = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({
        color: 0x000000,
        roughness: 0.35,
        metalness: 0.0,
        side: THREE.DoubleSide,
        map: crackTexture.getVisibleTexture(), // Visible cracks on black surface
        // No alphaMap, no transparent - cracks are just color on the surface
    });
    
    const face = new THREE.Mesh(faceGeometry, faceMaterial);
    face.position.set(...data.pos);
    face.rotation.set(...data.rot);
    face.userData = { 
        faceIndex: index, 
        originalPosition: data.pos, 
        originalRotation: data.rot,
        clickCount: 0,
        maxClicks: 4,
        crackTexture: crackTexture,
    };
    scene.add(face);
    faces.push(face);
});

// Wireframe - create edges for the whole cube
const cubeGeometry = new THREE.BoxGeometry(faceSize, faceSize, faceSize);
const edges = new THREE.EdgesGeometry(cubeGeometry);
const lineMaterial = new THREE.LineBasicMaterial({ 
    color: 0x333333, 
    transparent: true, 
    opacity: 0.4 
});
const wireframe = new THREE.LineSegments(edges, lineMaterial);
scene.add(wireframe);

// Group for rotation (contains faces + wireframe)
const cubeGroup = new THREE.Group();
faces.forEach(face => cubeGroup.add(face));
cubeGroup.add(wireframe);
scene.add(cubeGroup);

// Lighting - multiple soft white lights
const ambientLight = new THREE.AmbientLight(0xffffff, 0.5);
scene.add(ambientLight);

const directionalLight = new THREE.DirectionalLight(0xffffff, 0.7);
directionalLight.position.set(5, 10, 7);
scene.add(directionalLight);

const fillLight = new THREE.DirectionalLight(0xffffff, 0.5);
fillLight.position.set(-5, 5, -7);
scene.add(fillLight);

const topLight = new THREE.DirectionalLight(0xffffff, 0.4);
topLight.position.set(0, 10, 0);
scene.add(topLight);

const sideLight1 = new THREE.DirectionalLight(0xffffff, 0.3);
sideLight1.position.set(10, 0, 5);
scene.add(sideLight1);

const sideLight2 = new THREE.DirectionalLight(0xffffff, 0.3);
sideLight2.position.set(-10, 0, -5);
scene.add(sideLight2);

// Raycaster for click detection
const raycaster = new THREE.Raycaster();
const mouse = new THREE.Vector2();

// Click handler
function onClick(event) {
    mouse.x = (event.clientX / window.innerWidth) * 2 - 1;
    mouse.y = -(event.clientY / window.innerHeight) * 2 + 1;

    raycaster.setFromCamera(mouse, camera);
    const intersects = raycaster.intersectObjects(faces.filter(f => f.visible), true);

    if (intersects.length > 0) {
        const hitFace = intersects[0].object;
        handleFaceClick(hitFace);
    }
}

function handleFaceClick(face) {
    const userData = face.userData;
    userData.clickCount++;
    
    // Add more cracks with each click
    // Click 1: 3 cracks, Click 2: 5 cracks, Click 3: 8 cracks, Click 4: shatter
    const cracksToAdd = [3, 5, 8, 0][userData.clickCount - 1] || 0;
    if (cracksToAdd > 0) {
        userData.crackTexture.addCracks(cracksToAdd);
    }
    
    // If 4th click, animate shatter disappearance
    if (userData.clickCount >= userData.maxClicks) {
        animateFaceShatter(face);
    }
}

function animateFaceShatter(face) {
    const userData = face.userData;
    const crackTexture = userData.crackTexture;
    
    // Create shatter pieces from the crack pattern
    const pieces = createShatterPieces(face, crackTexture);
    
    // Animate pieces flying off
    const startTime = performance.now();
    const duration = 600;
    
    function animatePieces() {
        const elapsed = performance.now() - startTime;
        const progress = Math.min(elapsed / duration, 1);
        const eased = Math.pow(progress, 2); // easeInQuad
        
        pieces.forEach((piece, i) => {
            const delay = i * 0.02;
            const pieceProgress = Math.max(0, (progress - delay) / (1 - delay));
            if (pieceProgress <= 0) return;
            
            const pieceEased = Math.pow(pieceProgress, 2);
            
            // Fly outward and rotate
            piece.position.x += piece.userData.velocity.x * pieceEased * 0.5;
            piece.position.y += piece.userData.velocity.y * pieceEased * 0.5;
            piece.position.z += piece.userData.velocity.z * pieceEased * 0.5;
            
            piece.rotation.x += piece.userData.angularVelocity.x * pieceEased;
            piece.rotation.y += piece.userData.angularVelocity.y * pieceEased;
            piece.rotation.z += piece.userData.angularVelocity.z * pieceEased;
            
            // Fade out
            piece.material.opacity = 1 - pieceEased;
            piece.material.transparent = true;
            piece.material.needsUpdate = true;
        });
        
        if (progress < 1) {
            requestAnimationFrame(animatePieces);
        } else {
            // Cleanup
            pieces.forEach(piece => {
                scene.remove(piece);
                piece.geometry.dispose();
                piece.material.dispose();
            });
            face.visible = false;
            // Reset crack texture for potential reuse
            crackTexture.clear();
            userData.clickCount = 0;
        }
    }
    
    // Hide original face immediately
    face.visible = false;
    animatePieces();
}

function createShatterPieces(face, crackTexture) {
    const pieces = [];
    const pieceCount = 12 + Math.floor(Math.random() * 8);
    
    // Get face world position and rotation
    const worldPos = new THREE.Vector3();
    const worldQuat = new THREE.Quaternion();
    face.getWorldPosition(worldPos);
    face.getWorldQuaternion(worldQuat);
    
    for (let i = 0; i < pieceCount; i++) {
        // Create irregular piece geometry (slightly randomized box)
        const size = 0.15 + Math.random() * 0.15;
        const geo = new THREE.BoxGeometry(size, size, 0.02);
        
        // Shatter piece material: visible cracks + alpha transparency for crack holes
        const mat = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({
            color: 0x000000,
            roughness: 0.35,
            metalness: 0.0,
            side: THREE.DoubleSide,
            map: crackTexture.getVisibleTexture(),      // Visible gray cracks
            alphaMap: crackTexture.getAlphaTexture(),   // Transparent crack holes
            transparent: true,
            alphaTest: 0.01,
        });
        mat.needsUpdate = true;
        
        const piece = new THREE.Mesh(geo, mat);
        
        // Position near face center with small offset
        const offset = new THREE.Vector3(
            (Math.random() - 0.5) * 0.8,
            (Math.random() - 0.5) * 0.8,
            0
        );
        offset.applyQuaternion(worldQuat);
        piece.position.copy(worldPos).add(offset);
        piece.quaternion.copy(worldQuat);
        
        // Random velocity outward
        const velocity = new THREE.Vector3(
            (Math.random() - 0.5) * 0.02,
            (Math.random() - 0.5) * 0.02,
            (Math.random() - 0.5) * 0.01 - 0.005 // Slight backward
        );
        velocity.applyQuaternion(worldQuat);
        
        // Random angular velocity
        const angularVelocity = new THREE.Vector3(
            (Math.random() - 0.5) * 0.05,
            (Math.random() - 0.5) * 0.05,
            (Math.random() - 0.5) * 0.05
        );
        
        piece.userData = { velocity, angularVelocity };
        scene.add(piece);
        pieces.push(piece);
    }
    
    return pieces;
}

window.addEventListener('click', onClick);

// Minimal Audio Engine
class AudioEngine {
    constructor() {
        this.ctx = new (window.AudioContext || window.webkitAudioContext)();
        this.masterGain = this.ctx.createGain();
        this.masterGain.connect(this.ctx.destination);
        this.masterGain.gain.value = 0.15;
        this.isPlaying = false;
    }

    resume() {
        if (this.ctx.state === 'suspended') this.ctx.resume();
    }

    start() {
        if (this.isPlaying) return;
        this.isPlaying = true;
        this.scheduleLoop();
    }

    stop() {
        this.isPlaying = false;
    }

    scheduleLoop() {
        if (!this.isPlaying) return;
        
        const now = this.ctx.currentTime;
        const bpm = 70;
        const beatInterval = 60 / bpm;
        
        for (let i = 0; i < 4; i++) {
            const time = now + i * beatInterval;
            this.scheduleBeat(time, i % 4);
        }
        
        setTimeout(() => this.scheduleLoop(), beatInterval * 4 * 1000 * 0.95);
    }

    scheduleBeat(time, beatIndex) {
        if (beatIndex % 2 === 0) {
            const kick = this.ctx.createOscillator();
            const kickGain = this.ctx.createGain();
            kick.connect(kickGain);
            kickGain.connect(this.masterGain);
            kick.frequency.setValueAtTime(60, time);
            kick.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(30, time + 0.8);
            kickGain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.8, time);
            kickGain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, time + 0.8);
            kick.start(time);
            kick.stop(time + 1);
        }

        if (beatIndex === 0) {
            this.playPad(time);
        }

        if (beatIndex % 2 === 1) {
            this.playHihat(time);
        }
    }

    playPad(time) {
        const frequencies = [65.41, 82.41, 98.00, 130.81];
        frequencies.forEach((freq) => {
            const osc = this.ctx.createOscillator();
            const gain = this.ctx.createGain();
            const filter = this.ctx.createBiquadFilter();
            osc.connect(filter);
            filter.connect(gain);
            gain.connect(this.masterGain);
            
            osc.type = 'sine';
            osc.frequency.value = freq;
            filter.type = 'lowpass';
            filter.frequency.value = 400;
            filter.Q.value = 2;
            
            gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0, time);
            gain.gain.linearRampToValueAtTime(0.05, time + 0.5);
            gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, time + 6);
            
            osc.start(time);
            osc.stop(time + 7);
        });
    }

    playHihat(time) {
        const noise = this.ctx.createBufferSource();
        const bufferSize = this.ctx.sampleRate * 0.03;
        const buffer = this.ctx.createBuffer(1, bufferSize, this.ctx.sampleRate);
        const data = buffer.getChannelData(0);
        for (let i = 0; i < bufferSize; i++) data[i] = (Math.random() * 2 - 1) * 0.1;
        noise.buffer = buffer;
        
        const gain = this.ctx.createGain();
        const filter = this.ctx.createBiquadFilter();
        noise.connect(filter);
        filter.connect(gain);
        gain.connect(this.masterGain);
        
        filter.type = 'highpass';
        filter.frequency.value = 8000;
        gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.05, time);
        gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, time + 0.1);
        
        noise.start(time);
    }
}

const audioEngine = new AudioEngine();

function initAudio() {
    audioEngine.resume();
    if (!audioEngine.isPlaying) audioEngine.start();
    window.removeEventListener('click', initAudio);
    window.removeEventListener('keydown', initAudio);
}
window.addEventListener('click', initAudio);
window.addEventListener('keydown', initAudio);

// Resize
window.addEventListener('resize', () => {
    camera.aspect = window.innerWidth / window.innerHeight;
    camera.updateProjectionMatrix();
    renderer.setSize(window.innerWidth, window.innerHeight);
});

// Animation loop
function animate() {
    requestAnimationFrame(animate);
    
    controls.update();
    
    // Subtle cube rotation
    cubeGroup.rotation.x += 0.0005;
    cubeGroup.rotation.y += 0.0008;
    
    // Pulse wireframe opacity slightly
    const time = performance.now() * 0.001;
    wireframe.material.opacity = 0.3 + Math.sin(time * 0.5) * 0.1;
    
    renderer.render(scene, camera);
}

animate();

The user wants the cracks to be visible on the black glass cube. Currently, the cracks are drawn on a black background with gray lines, but since the cube face is also black, the cracks might not be visible enough. The issue is that the `map` texture is being applied to a black material (`color: 0x000000`), so the black parts of the texture blend in, and the gray cracks might be too subtle.

Let me think about how to make the cracks more visible on a black glass surface:

2. On black glass, cracks would appear as lighter lines (refracting light) with maybe some white highlights
3. The current approach uses a texture map, but the material color is black, so the texture multiplies with black = black

- Or use a combination of normal map + emissive for the crack effect
- Since it's "black glass", the cracks should be visible because they refract/reflect light differently

Actually, the simplest fix: make the visible texture have a dark gray background (e.g., #111111) instead of pure black, and make the cracks brighter (white/light gray with glow). Since the material color is 0x000000, the map will multiply - so we need the texture to be brighter than the material color. But material color is multiplied with map. If material is black (0,0,0), any map multiplied by it becomes black.
